P0-C PairTrusted / PairCorroborated offline impact
========================================================================
evidence class: OFFLINE REPLAY (recomputed from exports; no live plugin run)
implementation: independent Python re-run of Contribution.Compute (see module docstring)
  owner ladder : contrib.attribution.OwnerIndex.resolve
  fold vocab   : contrib.aggregate.extract_folds   split: contrib.aggregate.split_hit
  aggregation  : local loop mirroring src/Output/Contribution.cs Compute()

criteria:
  A   all calc.fold + calc.atkAdd (deployed Contribution.Compute)
  B   folds only when calc.pairTrusted == true (same event set / denominator)
  C   folds only when pairTrusted == true AND pairCorroborated == true (same denominator)
  B*  hits only when pairTrusted == true, all their folds (DIFFERENT denominator)
  C*  hits only when pairTrusted && pairCorroborated, all their folds (DIFFERENT denominator)
  A/B/C keep the same event set and denominator; only the fold trust varies.
  B*/C* (marked) drop whole hits -> DIFFERENT denominator, not rank-comparable with A.
